### Mz. XVIII.

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>降落来</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>执掌</rb><rt>tsih-công</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>光明</rb><rt>kwông-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>讴出来</rb><rt>eo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>跌倒了</rb><rt>Tih-tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴比伦</rb><rt>Pô-pi-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>跌倒了</rb><rt>tih-tao-de</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>做了</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>庉个</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>邪灵个</rb><rt>zia-ling-go</rt><rb>巢穴</rb><rt>dzao-yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>呕憎</rb><rt>eo-tseng</rt><rb>鸟个</rb><rt>tiao-go</rt><rb>巢穴</rb><rt>dzao-yüih</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>万国</rb><rt>væn-koh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>淫乱</rb><rt>ying-lön</rt><rb>触怒个</rb><rt>cʽoh-nu-go</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>地上个</rb><rt>di-zông-go</rt><rb>君王</rb><rt>kyüing-wông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>地上个</rb><rt>di-zông-go</rt><rb>客商</rb><rt>kʽah-shông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奢华</rb><rt>shæ-wô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>极头</rb><rt>gyih-deo</rt><rb>发财了</rb><rt>fah-dzæ-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>灾难</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt></ruby>；<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>罪恶</rb><rt>ze-ôh</rt><rb>滔</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>坏处</rb><rt>w̆a-cʽü</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>Tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>加倍</rb><rt>kô-be</rt><rb>报</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>杯子里</rb><rt>pe-ts-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>加倍</rb><rt>kô-be</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>尊重</rb><rt>tseng-djong</rt><rb>奢华</rb><rt>shæ-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>照数</rb><rt>tsiao-su</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>痛苦</rb><rt>tʽong-kwʽu</rt><rb>伤心</rb><rt>sông-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王后</rb><rt>wông-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>寡妇</rb><rt>kwô-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>悲伤</rb><rt>pe-shông</rt></ruby>。』<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>灾难</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>横死</rb><rt>wang-si</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>痛伤</rb><rt>tʽong-sông</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>饥荒</rb><rt>kyi-hwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>𤈦煞</rb><rt>me-sah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>审判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9, 10</sup> <ruby><rb>地上个</rb><rt>Di-zông-go</rt><rb>君王</rb><rt>kyüing-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>奢华个</rb><rt>shæ-wô-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>困苦</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kwʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>远远</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>火着个</rb><rt>ho-dziah-go</rt><rb>烟</rb><rt>in</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>哀痛</rb><rt>æ-tʽong</rt><rb>啼哭</rb><rt>di-kʽoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>可惜</rb><rt>Kʽo-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可惜</rb><rt>kʽo-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴比伦</rb><rt>Pô-pi-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>坚固个</rb><rt>kyin-kwu-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>一时里</rb><rt>ih-z-li</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>审判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] 此处原书错印为 shæ-wô-gô</sup>

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>地上个</rb><rt>Di-zông-go</rt><rb>客商</rb><rt>kʽah-shông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>哀痛</rb><rt>æ-tʽong</rt><rb>啼哭</rb><rt>di-kʽoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>买</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>货物</rb><rt>ho-veh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>货物</rb><rt>ho-veh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>银</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>宝石</rb><rt>pao-zah</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>珠子</rb><rt>cü-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紫红</rb><rt>ts-ʽong</rt><rb>丝线</rb><rt>s-sin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>百样</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>香木</rb><rt>hyiang-moh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>象牙</rb><rt>ziang-ngô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>贵重</rb><rt>kwe-djong</rt><rb>木头</rb><rt>moh-deo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>铜</rb><rt>dong</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>铁</rb><rt>tʽih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>玉石个</rb><rt>nyüoh-zah-go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt></ruby>；<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>肉桂</rb><rt>nyüoh-kwe</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>香料</rb><rt>hyiang-liao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>香油</rb><rt>hyiang-yiu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>乳香</rb><rt>jü-hyiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>细</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>面粉</rb><rt>min-feng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>麦</rb><rt>mah</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>马</rb><rt>mô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>车子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>活灵</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>企慕个</rb><rt>kyʽi-mo-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>奢华</rb><rt>shæ-wô</rt><rb>艳丽个</rb><rt>yin-li-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>卖</rb><rt>Ma</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>发财</rb><rt>fah-dzæ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>客商</rb><rt>kʽah-shông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>困苦</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kwʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>远远</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>哀痛</rb><rt>æ-tʽong</rt><rb>啼哭</rb><rt>di-kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>可惜</rb><rt>Kʽo-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可惜</rb><rt>kʽo-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>细</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>紫红</rb><rt>ts-ʽong</rt><rb>颜色</rb><rt>ngæn-seh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>宝石</rb><rt>pao-zah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>珠子</rb><rt>cü-ts</rt><rb>装饰个</rb><rt>tsông-sih-go</rt></ruby>！<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>多个</rb><rt>to-go</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>一时里</rb><rt>ih-z-li</rt><rb>灭掉了</rb><rt>mih-diao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>船主</rb><rt>jün-cü</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>水手</rb><rt>shü-siu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>驶过</rb><rt>s-ko</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生意</rb><rt>sang-i</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>远远</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt></ruby>，<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>火着个</rb><rt>ho-dziah-go</rt><rb>烟</rb><rt>in</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴响</rb><rt>eo-hyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>！』」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>灰尘</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt><rb>戴勒</rb><rt>tæ-leh</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>哀痛</rb><rt>æ-tʽong</rt><rb>啼哭</rb><rt>di-kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>可惜</rb><rt>Kʽo-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可惜</rb><rt>kʽo-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>奢华</rb><rt>shæ-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>发财</rb><rt>fah-dzæ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>一时里</rb><rt>ih-z-li</rt><rb>灭掉了</rb><rt>mih-diao-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>伸冤了</rb><rt>sing-ün-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>能干个</rb><rt>neng-ken-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>磨盘</rb><rt>mo-bun</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>丢勒</rb><rt>tiu-leh</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>顶大</rb><rt>Ting-do</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴比伦</rb><rt>Pô-pi-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>必然</rb><rt>pih-jün</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>登时</rb><rt>teng-z</rt><rb>倒崩</rb><rt>tao-pong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>太阳里</rb><rt>tʽa-yiang-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>天空里</rb><rt>tʽin-kʽong-li</rt><rb>飞个</rb><rt>fi-go</rt><rb>百鸟</rb><rt>pah-tiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>酒筵</rb><rt>tsiu-yin</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>；<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>君王个</rb><rt>kyüing-wông-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>将军个</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>英雄个</rb><rt>ing-yüong-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>马</rb><rt>mô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>骑马</rb><rt>gyi-mô</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>众人个</rb><rt>cong-nying-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>大大小小</rb><rt>do-do-siao-siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>野兽</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地上个</rb><rt>di-zông-go</rt><rb>君王</rb><rt>kyüing-wông</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>兵将</rb><rt>ping-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>骑马</rb><rt>gyi-mô</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>兵将</rb><rt>ping-tsiang</rt><rb>交战</rb><rt>kao-tsin</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>雪白</rb><rt>shih-bah</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>坐的个</rb><rt>zo-tih-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>避过</rb><rt>bi-ko</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>呒处</rb><rt>m-cʽü</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>死过了</rb><rt>si-ko-liao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>小小</rb><rt>siao-siao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>册子</rb><rt>tsʽah-ts</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>摊开</rb><rt>tʽæn-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一本</rb><rt>ih-peng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>活命个</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>册子</rb><rt>tsʽah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>摊开</rb><rt>tʽæn-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>册子里</rb><rt>tsʽah-ts-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>写的个</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>Hæ</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>交出</rb><rt>kao-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>坟墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>交出</rb><rt>kao-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>死</rb><rt>Si</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>坟墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>丢</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>火坑里</rb><rt>ho-kʽang-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>第二遭</rb><rt>di-nyi-tsao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>册子里</rb><rt>tsʽah-ts-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>丢</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>火坑里</rb><rt>ho-kʽang-li</rt></ruby>。

### Mz. XXI.

<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>簇新个</rb><rt>tsʽoh-sing-go</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>簇新个</rb><rt>tsʽoh-sing-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>簇新个</rb><rt>tsʽoh-sing-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>降落来</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>备办</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>新妇</rb><rt>sing-vu</rt><rb>装饰</rb><rt>tsông-sih</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>揩燥</rb><rt>kʽa-sao</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>眼泪</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>以后</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>伤心</rb><rt>sông-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>啼哭</rb><rt>di-kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>劳苦</rb><rt>lao-kwʽu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>过去了</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>坐</rb><rt>Zo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>新造</rb><rt>sing-zao</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>忠信</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt><rb>诚实个</rb><rt>dzing-jih-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>完了</rb><rt>Wun-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>亚勒巴</rb><rt>Üô-leh-pô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿米加</rb><rt>O-mi-kyüô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>结煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>口渴个</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>白白</rb><rt>bah-bah</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>活命个</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>水源</rb><rt>shü-nyün</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>得胜个</rb><rt>Teh-sing-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>万物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>胆怯个</rb><rt>tæn-kyʽiah-go</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>可恶个</rb><rt>kʽo-u-go</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>凶手</rb><rt>hyüong-siu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>邪术</rb><rt>zia-jih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>假个</rb><rt>kô-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>硫磺</rb><rt>liu-wông</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>着个</rb><rt>dziah-go</rt><rb>坑里</rb><rt>kʽang-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>第二遭</rb><rt>di-nyi-tsao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>七个</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>七个</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>香炉</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>𣥼满</rb><rt>tsi-mun</rt><rb>结煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt><rb>七样</rb><rt>tsʽih-yiang</rt><rb>灾难</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其中</rb><rt>gyi-cong</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>新妇</rb><rt>sing-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>小羊个</rb><rt>Siao-yiang-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>蒙</rb><rt>mong</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*sing*-ling</rt><rb>感动</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>高大个</rb><rt>kao-do-go</rt><rb>山顶</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>降落来</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>贵重</rb><rt>kwe-djong</rt><rb>宝石</rb><rt>pao-zah</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>梗青个</rb><rt>kwang-tsʽing-go</rt><rb>碧玉</rb><rt>pih-nyüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>城墙</rb><rt>dzing-ziang</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>城门</rb><rt>dzing-meng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十二道</rb><rt>jih-nyi-dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>门上</rb><rt>meng-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>支派个</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>东边</rb><rt>Tong-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>西边</rb><rt>si-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>Dzing-ts</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十二埭</rb><rt>jih-nyi-da</rt><rb>墙脚</rb><rt>ziang-kyiah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>小羊个</rb><rt>Siao-yiang-go</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>金子个</rb><rt>kying-ts-go</rt><rb>丈尺</rb><rt>dziang-tsʽih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>量</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>城门</rb><rt>dzing-meng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>城墙</rb><rt>dzing-ziang</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>Dzing-ts</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt><rb>转方</rb><rt>cün-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>长</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阔</rb><rt>kwʽeh</rt><rb>一色斯样</rb><rt>ih-seh-s-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>丈尺</rb><rt>dziang-tsʽih</rt><rb>量量</rb><rt>liang-liang</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt><rb>里</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>长</rb><rt>Dziang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阔</rb><rt>kwʽeh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>一样个</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>量</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>城墙</rb><rt>dzing-ziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>四十四</rb><rt>s-jih-s</rt><rb>尺</rb><rt>tsʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>量度</rb><rt>liang-doh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>天使个</rb><rt>tʽin-s-go</rt><rb>量度</rb><rt>liang-doh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>城墙</rb><rt>Dzing-ziang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>碧玉</rb><rt>pih-nyüoh</rt><rb>叠成</rb><rt>diah-dzing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>Dzing-ts</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>赤金</rb><rt>tsʽih-kying</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>梗青个</rb><rt>kwang-tsʽing-go</rt><rb>玻璃</rb><rt>po-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>城子个</rb><rt>Dzing-ts-go</rt><rb>墙脚</rb><rt>ziang-kyiah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>宝石</rb><rt>pao-zah</rt><rb>装饰</rb><rt>*tsông-sih*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>第一埭</rb><rt>Di-ih-da</rt><rb>墙脚</rb><rt>ziang-kyiah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>碧玉</rb><rt>pih-nyüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>青玉</rb><rt>tsʽing-nyüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>苍玉</rb><rt>tsʽông-nyüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>第四</rb><rt>di-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>葱玉</rb><rt>tsʽong-nyüoh</rt></ruby>；<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>第五</rb><rt>di-ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>杂红玉</rb><rt>dzeh-ʽong-nyüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>第六</rb><rt>di-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>玛瑙</rb><rt>mô-nao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>黄玉</rb><rt>wông-nyüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>第八</rb><rt>di-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>绿玉</rb><rt>loh-nyüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>第九</rb><rt>di-kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>淡黄玉</rb><rt>dæn-wông-nyüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>第十</rb><rt>di-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>翡翠</rb><rt>fi-tsʽe</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>十一</rb><rt>jih-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赤玉</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>紫玉</rb><rt>ts-nyüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>十二道</rb><rt>jih-nyi-dao</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>十二颗</rb><rt>jih-nyi-kʽo</rt><rb>珠子</rb><rt>cü-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>城门</rb><rt>dzing-meng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一颗</rb><rt>ih-kʽo</rt><rb>珠子</rb><rt>cü-ts</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>城里个</rb><rt>zing-li-go</rt><rb>街道</rb><rt>ka-dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>赤金</rb><rt>tsʽih-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>梗青个</rb><rt>kwang-tsʽing-go</rt><rb>玻璃</rb><rt>po-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>里向</rb><rt>Li-hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>圣殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>全能个</rb><rt>djün-neng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>Siao-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>*Zi*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>圣殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>城里</rb><rt>Zing-li</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>月亮</rb><rt>yüih-liang</rt><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>透出</rb><rt>tʽeo-cʽih</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>Siao-yiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>灯盏</rb><rt>teng-tsæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>得救个</rb><rt>teh-kyiu-go</rt><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>行动</rb><rt>ʽang-dong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尊贵</rb><rt>tseng-kwe</rt><rb>进来</rb><rt>tsing-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>可恶</rb><rt>kʽo-u</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>走弗进</rb><rt>tseo-feh-tsing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>Siao-yiang</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>册子里</rb><rt>tsʽah-ts-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby>。

### Mz. XXII.

<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>水晶</rb><rt>se-tsing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>梗青个</rb><rt>kwang-tsʽing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小羊个</rb><rt>Siao-yiang-go</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>流出来</rb><rt>liu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大街</rb><rt>do-ka</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>河个</rb><rt>ʽo-go</rt><rb>两边</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>十二种</rb><rt>jih-nyi-cong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>树个</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>叶</rb><rt>yih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>国份个</rb><rt>koh-veng-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>以后</rb><rt>Yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>咒骂</rb><rt>tsiu-mô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小羊个</rb><rt>Siao-yiang-go</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>；<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>额角上</rb><rt>ngah-kôh-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>用着</rb><rt>yüong-djôh</rt><rb>灯盏</rb><rt>teng-tsæn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>日头个</rb><rt>nyih-deo-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>光照</rb><rt>kwông-tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>忠信</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt><rb>诚实个</rb><rt>dzing-jih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>圣善</rb><rt>Sing-jün</rt><rb>先知人个</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>应验</rb><rt>ing-nyiæn</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>告诉</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>速速</rb><rt>soh-soh</rt><rb>来其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>箇本</rb><rt>keh-peng</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>脚下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>使弗得</rb><rt>S-feh-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>箇本</rb><rt>keh-peng</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>同事个</rb><rt>dong-z-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>封好</rb><rt>fong-hao</rt><rb>箇本</rb><rt>keh-peng</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>先知个</rb><rt>sin-cü-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>近了</rb><rt>gying-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>不义</rb><rt>Peh-yi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>不义</rb><rt>peh-yi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>污秽</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>污秽</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>速速</rb><rt>soh-soh</rt><rb>来其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>报应</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>各人个</rb><rt>kôh-nying-go</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>报</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>亚勒巴</rb><rt>Üô-leh-pô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿米加</rb><rt>O-mi-kyüô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>前</rb><rt>zin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>后</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>结煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> 「<ruby><rb>守</rb><rt>Siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>号令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt><rb>活命个</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>进</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>外头</rb><rt>Nga-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>恶狗</rb><rt>ôh-keo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>邪术</rb><rt>zia-jih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>凶手</rb><rt>hyüong-siu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>根株</rb><rt>keng-cü</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇粒</rb><rt>keh-lih</rt><rb>明亮个</rb><rt>ming-liang-go</rt><rb>五更晓</rb><rt>ng-kang-hyiao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>新妇</rb><rt>sing-vu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>听闻个</rb><rt>Tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>口渴个</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>要个</rb><rt>iao-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>白白</rb><rt>bah-bah</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>活命个</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇本</rb><rt>keh-peng</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>加上</rb><rt>kô-zông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>加上</rb><rt>kô-zông</rt><rb>箇本</rb><rt>keh-peng</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>写的个</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt><rb>灾难</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>减落</rb><rt>kæn-lôh</rt><rb>箇本</rb><rt>keh-peng</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>先知个</rb><rt>sin-cü-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>减</rb><rt>kæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>册子</rb><rt>tsʽah-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>圣城里</rb><rt>sing-dzing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>箇本</rb><rt>keh-peng</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>写的个</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>许</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>对证</rb><rt>Te-tsing</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>速速</rb><rt>soh-soh</rt><rb>来其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亚们</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>临着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亚们</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。
